# Agents · V · part 5

1,062 entries, verifier to verify-frontend.eval. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **verifier**: Cross-phase artifact verifier. Invoked by the Orchestrator at each phase gate to confirm that all required artifacts exist on disk AND are internally complete. Returns a structured PASS or FAIL report with per-step granularity. Never modifies artifacts — read and inspect only. ([iGore/theseus](https://github.com/iGore/theseus/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs gates and decisive checks against an exact commit and reports evidence without repairing anything. Use for the final gate of a delivery. ([IgorGrandin/agentic-harness-claude](https://github.com/IgorGrandin/agentic-harness-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent verifier that tests acceptance criteria and reports decisive evidence without fixing failures. ([IgorGrandin/agentic-harness-v4](https://github.com/IgorGrandin/agentic-harness-v4/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/verifier.md) · 3 copies)
- **verifier**: Checks whether a claimed state or displayed number was actually established by the code, or is merely asserted. Use whenever the UI shows a dollar figure, a field status, or any confirmation. ([ihatecodingaaa/fairslip](https://github.com/ihatecodingaaa/fairslip/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Independent pre-merge verifier for one pull request. Give it the PR number, the head commit SHA, and what the change must do; it runs the change the way a user would, parent against head, and returns PASS, PASS+NOTES, or FAIL with evidence. Use before merging a PR the session authored. ([ImAno177/cstack](https://github.com/ImAno177/cstack/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Logical verification specialist. Finds contradictions, unsupported claims, and reasoning errors in documents. /bts-verify runs inside this agent. ([imtemp-dev/claude-bts](https://github.com/imtemp-dev/claude-bts/blob/HEAD/internal/template/templates/.claude/agents/bts-verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Logical verification specialist. Finds contradictions, unsupported claims, and reasoning errors in documents. ([imtemp-dev/imtemp-dev.github.io](https://github.com/imtemp-dev/imtemp-dev.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bts-verifier.md))
- **verifier**: The one verification agent, end to end. Reviews a diff cold against its stated scope, runs the repo's matching verify skill (with the E2E lanes it calls for) or scoped checks, and returns pass or fail with only the failing lines. No other role or agent does any part of verification. ([injaneity/zstack](https://github.com/injaneity/zstack/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Use this agent when a fixer on an agent team has submitted a locally committed SHA and the lead needs an evidence gate before any pull request is opened. Typical triggers include a fixer's submission message naming an issue, branch and SHA, a resubmission after REWORK, and a lead asking whether a teammate's commit is ready to ship. Read-only on GitHub; it never pushes, opens PRs, or comments. Not for implementing fixes (use the fixer). See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([Integral-Productivity/integral-productivity-engineering-claude-plugin](https://github.com/Integral-Productivity/integral-productivity-engineering-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the project's quality gates and checks a change against its plan before the work is reported done. Use after implementing any change, and always before claiming a task is complete. ([InYrDim/modular-monolith-laravel](https://github.com/InYrDim/modular-monolith-laravel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs typecheck / lint / tests / build and reports failures only. Tech-agnostic — auto-detects package manager and scripts. Use after edits, before claiming work is done. Never edits code. ([ionrusu114/case-modulare](https://github.com/ionrusu114/case-modulare/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: End-to-end verification agent for the aoppop pipeline. Runs the quality gate, checks target state, geometry validity, and population conservation, and reports PASS/FAIL/SKIP per check. Use proactively before committing. ([ipea/aoppop](https://github.com/ipea/aoppop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: Re-derives one named claim from the primary source, independently of whoever made it, and returns REVERIFIED, REFUTED or UNDECIDABLE with the command. - **verifier**: Cold, independent check of a finished non-trivial change, before saying done, fixed or works. Ce qu'un agent ne peut pas constater (mise en page visuelle, effet externe) est remonté en humanCheckRequired plutôt que faussement attesté. Lecture seule — vérifie, ne corrige pas. - **verifier**: Vérifie un ticket implémenté en mode `observé` — là où il n'y a pas de test automatisé. - **verifier**: 검증 전담(계획 검증 02-plan-verify · 완료 검토 04-review · 커밋 전 코드 리뷰). 계획·구현을 만든 에이전트와 다른 모델·새 컨텍스트에서 기획서 카드와 증거만 보고 판정한다. 코드·계획을 고치지 않는다. "계획 검증", "점검표", "완료 검토", "수용 기준 대조", "리뷰해줘" 요청에 쓴다. L-002 — 계획·구현·검증은 서로 다른 모델이 맡는다. ([SunWoo1213/Relationship](https://github.com/SunWoo1213/Relationship/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independently verifies that an implementation satisfies a aderx-dev spec. Runs the project's lint, typecheck and tests, maps every acceptance criterion to real evidence, and reports PASS, FAIL or UNVERIFIED. Use after building and before opening a PR. Never edits code. ([super-aderx/GoGoAderx](https://github.com/super-aderx/GoGoAderx/blob/HEAD/plugins/aderx-dev/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 구현이 끝난 뒤 spec 기준으로 검증할 때 사용한다. 코드를 고치지 않고 문제만 보고한다. ([supremehappy/myharness-marketplace](https://github.com/supremehappy/myharness-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/myharness/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: The harness verifier for one work item. ([suredesigntshirts/ccp_machine](https://github.com/suredesigntshirts/ccp_machine/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Readonly verification against APPROVED REQ (and BUG when applicable). Use after test-runner with orchestrator-provided SHA, test report, and waiver ledger. Epistemically isolated — does not trust implementer/reviewer narratives or fix code. ([sushilti80/specforge-engineering-team](https://github.com/sushilti80/specforge-engineering-team/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent check of a claim or artifact against an acceptance command. Fresh context, read-only, must reproduce the evidence itself. Use after any delegated change or numeric answer that matters, and before reporting done. ([SyntheticPrometheus/synthetic-prometheus](https://github.com/SyntheticPrometheus/synthetic-prometheus/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Checks a finished change. Never edits code. ([tahakeler/unreal_game](https://github.com/tahakeler/unreal_game/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs PaperPilot's standard pre-commit verification gate (TypeScript workspace) and reports pass/fail with exact numbers. ([taichiiiiiiii/automatic-paper-search](https://github.com/taichiiiiiiii/automatic-paper-search/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: Tries to refute one code-review finding before it is reported. Use once per candidate finding from the tmn-fdekit review skill, in parallel, before anything is posted. ([teamnebula-ai/tmn-fdekit](https://github.com/teamnebula-ai/tmn-fdekit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))

It reads the diff and the code around it and returns confirmed issues with evidence apart from suspicions. It is a code review only: it runs no builds or tests, so hand it a change that already builds and passes its tests. It never edits. - **verifier**: Use to decide independently whether a committed revision meets its spec. - **verifier**: Independently re-solves every question in a generated session with no knowledge of how it was written. - **verifier**: 코딩 에이전트가 작성한 Flutter/Dart 코드가 작업 명세서의 요구사항을 충족하는지, 구현 계획의 시그니처를 준수했는지, 기존 프로젝트와 호환되는지를 검증한다. Flutter/Dart 코드 품질 검증은 flutter-reviewer 에이전트가 별도로 수행하며, 이 에이전트는 "명세대로 만들었는가"만 본다. PASS/FAIL 판정과 이슈 목록을 반환한다. 코드를 직접 수정하지 않는 읽기 전용 에이전트다. ([voidreader/band-of-mercenaries](https://github.com/voidreader/band-of-mercenaries/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))

([Mharbulous/probanda-dev](https://github.com/Mharbulous/probanda-dev/blob/HEAD/plugins/researcher/agents/verify-claim.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **verify-claim**: Pushback-blind independent verifier for a single numeric or factual claim. Use it whenever a claim needs to be checked by something other than the context that produced it — before a number is written into docs/ or lab-notebook/, before an experiment's headline is quoted downstream, before a verdict flips, and ALWAYS when someone disputes a figure. Launch it with the claim and where its inputs live; never tell it that anyone disagreed, never forward the objection, never say which answer you are hoping for. It re-derives the number from source and reports CONFIRMED / REFUTED / UNVERIFIABLE. ([londrwus/orr-etf-reverse-engineering](https://github.com/londrwus/orr-etf-reverse-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verify-claim.md))
- **verify-claim-faithfulness**: A verification agent that independently checks, at the sentence level, whether each cited source actually SUPPORTS the prose claim attributed to it — distinct from whether the reference exists. For every (claim sentence, citation) pair it decomposes the sentence into atomic sub-claims (direction, magnitude, population, causal status), retrieves the cited source's actual text via the tier chain (Knowledge Graph full-text → Zotero PDF → open-access full text → abstract), localizes the supporting/contradicting passage, and emits a structured claim-faithfulness-audit.ndjson record per claim with… ([joshzyj/open-scholar-skill](https://github.com/joshzyj/open-scholar-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verify-claim-faithfulness.md) · ★193)
- **verify-claims**: Fact-checks a paragraph of text or a file path. Extracts every verifiable claim, searches for supporting references using parallel-search-mcp (prioritising arxiv, scientific papers, and reputable research blogs), runs all searches in parallel, annotates the original text with inline citation markers, and writes a full report to research/verify/. Use when verifying research claims before publishing. ([dsaad68/research-template](https://github.com/dsaad68/research-template/blob/HEAD/.apm/agents/verify-claims.agent.md))
- **verify-claims**: Use for fact-checking a local knowledge base against current web sources. Verifies tool names, API details, version-specific claims, and best-practice recommendations. PROACTIVELY use when the user asks whether documented content is still accurate. ([TheBitcoinBreakdown-95/claude-verify-claims](https://github.com/TheBitcoinBreakdown-95/claude-verify-claims/blob/HEAD/agents/verify-claims.md))
- **verify-claims**: Checks specific factual claims and reports CONFIRMED / REFUTED / UNSUPPORTED with evidence. Use whenever a set of claims needs checking before they are acted on, or reported - audit findings, a "this is already fixed" assertion, a summary another agent produced, a doc that asserts something about the live system. Pass MANY claims in one invocation rather than one per agent; it is built to batch. Read-only, so it cannot change what it is checking. ([UrbanLens/UrbanLens](https://github.com/UrbanLens/UrbanLens/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verify-claims.md))
- **verify-code**: 빌드·린트·타입체크·테스트 명령 실행과 연결 정합 점검으로 PASS/FAIL 판정 (수정 없음). MUST USE when: 구현·수정 후 병합·리뷰 전에 실제 명령 결과(rc·출력)와 import·시그니처 정합으로 통과 여부를 확정해야 할 때. OUTPUT: PASS/FAIL + 명령별 rc + 끊어진 연결 ([This-HW/hiway-kit](https://github.com/This-HW/hiway-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/common/agents/verify-code.md) · ★5)
- **verify-code-reviewer**: Read-only code-review persona for /verify --deep; reviews completed work against the local code-reviewer contract without shadowing plugin code-reviewer agents. ([0xmariowu/builders-dont-cry](https://github.com/0xmariowu/builders-dont-cry/blob/HEAD/agents/verify-code-reviewer.md) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **verify-codebase**: You receive a set of PR review findings from Agents C and E, plus suggested fixes. Your job is to verify that the suggestions are consistent with how this specific codebase actually works — its patterns, conventions, idioms, and architectural decisions. ([huynguyenh/skills](https://github.com/huynguyenh/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/hnh-review-pr/agents/verify-codebase.md) · ★3)
- **verify-complete**: MDT completion verification agent for validating ticket implementation against requirements and quality checks. ([andkirby/markdown-ticket](https://github.com/andkirby/markdown-ticket/blob/HEAD/prompts/mdt/agents/verify-complete.md) · ★7)
- **verify-completeness**: A verification agent that ensures all artifacts (raw outputs, manuscript tables, manuscript figures) are properly cross-referenced, sequentially numbered, and complete. - **verify-compliance**: 요구사항 준수 검증 SubAgent. 현재 구현된 Phase를 파악하고, 해당 Phase 설계 문서(temp_docs/phase-N.md)의 완료 기준 체크리스트를 구현 파일과 대조하여 충족 여부를 확인한다. ([ydh1305/SampleOrderSystem-Andrew](https://github.com/ydh1305/SampleOrderSystem-Andrew/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verify-compliance.md))
- **verify-decompose**: Complete structural and semantic closure gate for DECOMPOSE. Reads plan + decompose index/shards from ALLOW READ. Never edits; no pytest. ([aeroalgo/dev-hub](https://github.com/aeroalgo/dev-hub/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/verify-decompose.md))
- **verify-delegation**: Black-box verify a delegated worker claim from the artifact, fixed-point inputs, success criteria, and independently reproducible read-only checks; never trust the worker narrative. ([quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk](https://github.com/quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/platform/adapters/claude/agents/foundation-verify-delegation.md))
- **verify-deployment**: Use this agent after deploying a service to verify it is live and healthy. Checks pod status and curls the public URL. Reports exact pass/fail output. ([sammasak/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/sammasak/claude-code-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/verify-deployment.md) · ★2)
- **verify-design**: Verifies that game design is coherent and complete. ([sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins](https://github.com/sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/game-design/agents/verify-design.md) · ★19)
- **verify-docs**: 문서 정합성 검증 SubAgent. CLAUDE.md, PRD.md, SPEC.md, docs/features/*.md, temp_docs/*.md 간 충돌·불일치를 검출한다. 클래스명·인터페이스 시그니처·주문 상태 전이·생산량 공식이 모든 문서에서 일관되는지 확인한다. - **verify-domain**: Domain audit board (sonnet). - **verify-e2e**: Gebruikt de draaiende app in een browser zoals een gebruiker, op 9 jaar nepdata, en meldt per stap geslaagd of niet met screenshots. ([Mharbulous/probanda-dev](https://github.com/Mharbulous/probanda-dev/blob/HEAD/plugins/lawyer/agents/verify-edges.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **verify-engineer**: Verification specialist for poc-lis-dignitas. Runs type checks, lint, and formatting on demand or after changes. - **verify-engineer**: Ingeniero de verificacion y deteccion. Exige evidencia objetiva; rechaza "deberia", "probablemente" o "parece". Degrada con honestidad cuando no hay tests, en vez de fingir verificacion.
